Frozen model · development benchmark · Newton HPC

Can LLaDA solve a 20-city TSP?

Valid tours when missing brackets are accepted: 1 out of 100.

Original LLaDA-8B-Instruct, before TSP fine-tuning. 100 cases · 64 diffusion steps · 128-token blocks. One answer per instance. No route repair or best-of-many selection.

Read all raw answers · Dataset and prompts · Detailed measurements

Read both scores: the frozen primary parser scores 0/100 because it requires a bracketed list. A separate punctuation-tolerant audit finds 1/100 valid tours and 0/100 within 5% of optimum. Primary scores below are unchanged.

Within 5% · primary parser0%0/100; 95% Wilson interval 0.0–3.7%
Mean gap · primary parser—Invalid answers are excluded only from this conditional metric.

Comparison on the same instances

MethodValidValid & ≤5% gapMean gap, validMedian gap, validMean solving time
LLaDA-8B-Instruct0/100 0/100 (0%) —— 13.7785 s
Nearest neighbor100/100 13/100 (13%) 17.36%15.79% 0.0001 s
Nearest neighbor + 2-opt100/100 88/100 (88%) 1.78%0.26% 0.0008 s

LLaDA time measures 64 GPU diffusion passes with synchronization. Classical methods run on CPU. Model download, loading, reference solving, and report creation are excluded. These are different algorithms and hardware, so latency is descriptive.

How to interpret this first test

This is a baseline for deciding what TSP fine-tuning must improve. Success in the next experiment means more valid tours and a higher valid-and-within-5% rate on a separate held-out set, under the same inference budget.

A valid tour contains every city exactly once before explicitly returning to its starting city. Rotations and reverse directions are accepted. No missing city, duplicate, open route, or unparseable answer is repaired. A unique unlabelled bracketed list can be accepted semantically; exact requested output formatting occurred in 0/100 answers.

Gap = 100 × (model tour length / optimal tour length − 1). Lengths use unrounded Euclidean distances. Every reference was solved by degree-2 mixed-integer optimization with subtour elimination, a connected solution, HiGHS status 0, and relative tolerance 10−9. This is a numerical optimality certificate.

Invalid-answer breakdown: unparseable or ambiguous route: 100

This small synthetic development set is not a published benchmark result or a held-out estimate after tuning. It does not establish whether diffusion models outperform autoregressive models.

Did formatting or decoding explain the failures?

This is a post-run diagnostic. The primary scores above retain the original parser and settings.

CheckMain run, 100 casesDiagnostic, 5 cases
Primary valid tours0/1000/5
Accept missing brackets1/1000/5
Accept missing brackets: valid & ≤5% gap0/1000/5
Accept missing brackets: mean gap on valid tours124.61%—
Full output canvas; accept missing brackets1/1000/5

The relaxed check accepts a complete comma-separated integer sequence without square brackets. It still requires all 20 cities once and an explicit return to the start; it never repairs a route. In the main run, 92 answers contain repeated visits and 82 omit cities among the sequences this check can extract. These categories overlap.

The primary answer stops before the first EOS/EOT token. The full-canvas check removes special-token markers without truncating; 0/100 main answers have different remaining text.

Five-case decoding diagnostic

Requested after observing repetitive primary outputs, using the first five instances in dataset order. Same model, prompts, seed, 128-token output and greedy decoding, with 128 passes and 32-token blocks, matching the settings in the official example. The local sampler is an adaptation, not a byte-for-byte upstream reproduction.

Primary validity: 0/5. Mean inference time: 27.52 s. Newton job 1457259 on newton4. This small diagnostic changes two decoding parameters together; it cannot isolate either one's effect or establish a reliable population score.

The main result applies to this prompt and decoding configuration. It is not a general verdict on LLaDA or diffusion models. Before SFT, a useful next study is a small, separately labelled prompt/decoding check and then a locked held-out evaluation set.

Example routes

The first three instances in dataset order, selected without reference to model performance. An invalid answer is shown as points without a scored route.

Reproducible setup

100 independent TSP20 instances; distinct integer coordinates in [0, 999]². Dataset seed 20261021. Each prompt includes coordinates and two nearest-neighbor hints, with no solution labels.

64 diffusion steps · 128 generated tokens · block length 128 · greedy temperature 0 · low-confidence remasking · classifier-free guidance 0 · BF16 · batch size 1.

Newton job 1457247 on newton4, NVIDIA A40. Peak model-run allocated GPU memory: 15.50 GiB. Python 3.10.12, PyTorch 2.6.0+cu124, Transformers 4.38.2.

Checkpoint: GSAI-ML/LLaDA-8B-Instruct, revision 08b83a6feb34df1a6011b80c3c00c7563e963b07.
Dataset SHA-256: b13ca290d96b6e6a06979695ecd32ef1767fab9af6454a1c8faf8e733d0386b1.

Protocol inspired by LLMCoSolver; this is an independent dataset and experiment, not a reproduction of its reported scores. Sampler adapted from the official LLaDA sampler.

Accompanying files: dataset.json, config.json, runtime.json, all_results.json, results.csv, summary.json, and figure files. Raw answers and full generated token canvases are retained.
